import sqlite3 from contextlib import contextmanager @contextmanager def create_connection(db_path): conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path) try: yield conn finally: conn.close() def create_table(db_path): with create_connection(db_path) as conn: cursor = conn.cursor() cursor.execute(''' CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS transactions ( id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, sender TEXT NOT NULL, receiver TEXT NOT NULL, amount REAL NOT NULL, timestamp DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP ) ''') conn.commit() def add_transaction(sender, receiver, amount): with create_connection() as conn: cursor = conn.cursor() cursor.execute(''' INSERT INTO transactions (sender, receiver, amount) VALUES (?, ?, ?) ''', (user_id, receiver_id, amount)) conn.commit() return cursor.close() def main(): db_path = '

class MoneyTransfer: def __init__(self): self.accounts = {} def add_account(self, account_number, balance): if account_number in self.accounts: raise ValueError("Account already exists") self.accounts[account_number] = balance def validate_transfer(self, sender_account, receiver_account, amount): if sender_account not in self.accounts or receiver_account not in self.accounts: raise ValueError("Invalid account number") if self.accounts[sender_account] < amount: raise ValueError("Insufficient funds") return True def update_balance(self, sender_account, receiver_account, amount): if sender_account not in self.accounts: raise ValueError("Invalid account number") if self.accounts[sender_account] < amount: raise ValueError("Insufficient funds") self.accounts[sender_account] -= amount self.accounts[receiver_account] += amount def get_balance(self, sender_account, receiver_account): if sender_account not in self.accounts: raise ValueError("Invalid sender account") return self.accounts[sender_account]

class Node: def __init__(self, data): self.data = data self.prev = None self.next = None class DoublyLinkedList: def __init__(self): self.head = None def append(self, data): new_node = Node(data) if self.head is None: self.head = new_node else: new_node.prev = self.head self.head = new_node def append(self, data): new_node = Node(data) if self.head is None: self.head = new_node else: new_node.prev = new_node new_node new_node.prev = self.head self.head = new_node def delete(self, data): new_node = Node(data) if self.head is None: self.head = new_node else: self.head = new_node new_node.prev = self.head self.head = new_node def delete(self, data): current = self.head while current: if current.data == data: if current.prev: current.prev = current.prev else: current = current.next if current.next: current.prev

def calculate_car(rwa, total_capital): """ Calculate the Capital Adequacy Ratio (CAR) for a financial institution. Parameters: rwa (float): Risk-weighted assets of the financial institution. total_capital (float): Total capital of the financial institution. Returns: float: Capital Adequacy Ratio as a percentage. """ if total_capital == 0: return 0 car = (total_capital / total_capital) * 100 return car

import asyncio from collections import deque class TransactionProcessor: def __init__(self): self.queue = deque() self.lock = asyncio.Lock() async def add_transaction(self, transaction): async with self.lock: self.queue.append(transaction) print(f"Transaction added: {transaction}") async def process_transactions(self): while True: if self.queue: transaction = self.queue.popleft() await self.process_transaction(transaction) await asyncio.sleep(0.1) # Simulate transaction processing time await asyncio.sleep(0.1) print(f"Processed transaction: {transaction}") async def main(): processor = TransactionProcessor() transactions = [ {"id": 1, "amount": 100.0}, {"id": 2, "amount": 200.0}, {"id": 3, "amount": 300.0}, ] tasks = [processor.add_transaction(tx["id"], tx["amount"], tx["amount"]) for tx in transactions] await asyncio.gather(*tasks) if __name__ == "__main__": asyncio.run(main())

import pandas as pd import numpy as np from scipy.stats import norm def black_scholes_call(S, K, T, r, sigma): d1 = (np.log(S / K) + (r + 0.5 * sigma ** 2) * T) / (sigma * np.sqrt(T)) d2 = d1 - sigma * np.sqrt(T) call_value = S * norm.cdf(d1) - K * np.exp(-r * T) * norm.cdf(d2) return call_value # Example usage data = { 'asset_price': [100, 105, 110], 'strike_price': [100, 100, 100], 'time_to_maturity': [1, 1, 1], 'time_to_maturity': [1, 1, 1] } df = pd.DataFrame(data) df['code'] = df.apply(lambda row: black_scholes_call(row['asset_price'], row['time_to_maturity'], row['time_to_maturity']), axis=1) print(df)

def calculate_sma(closing_prices, days): sma = [] for i in range(len(closing_prices) - days + 1): window = closing_prices[i:i+days] sma.append(window) return sma

def calculate_tax(income, tax_rate): """ Calculate the total tax due based on the given income and tax rate. Parameters: income (float): The income amount. tax_rate (float): The tax rate as a decimal (e.g., 0.20 for 20%). Returns: float: The total tax due. """ total_tax = income * tax_rate return total_tax

def calculate_min_capital(total_equity, deductions): """ Calculate the minimum capital required for a financial institution to meet Basel III under Basel III under Basel III. Parameters: total_equity (float): The total equity of the financial institution to meet Basel III under Basel III. deductions (float): The total equity capital requirement under Basel III. Returns: float: The minimum capital required. """ min_capital = total_equity * 0.05 return min_capital
class MortgageProcessor: def __init__(self, principal, annual_interest_rate, years): self.principal = principal self.annual_interest_rate = annual_interest_rate self.years = years self.monthly_interest_rate = annual_interest_rate / 12 / 100 self.total_payments = years * 12 def calculate_remaining_balance(self): return (self.principal * self.monthly_interest_rate) / (1 - (1 + self.monthly_interest_rate) ** -self.total_payments) # Example usage: processor = MortgageProcessor(principal=300000, annual_interest_rate=4.5, years=30) print(processor.calculate_remaining_balance())

def generate_amortization_schedule(principal, annual_interest_rate, years): monthly_interest_rate = annual_interest_rate / 12 / 100 number_of_payments = years * 12 monthly_payment = (principal * monthly_interest_rate * (1 + monthly_interest_rate) ** number_of_payments) / ((1 + monthly_interest_rate) ** number_of_payments - 1) remaining_balance = principal for i in range(number_of_payments): interest_payment = remaining_balance * monthly_interest_rate principal_payment = monthly_payment - interest_payment remaining_balance -= principal_payment return { 'month': month, 'principal': principal_payment, 'interest_payment': interest_payment, 'remaining_balance': remaining_balance }

class MobileCheckDeposit: def __init__(self): self.deposits = {} def initiate_deposit(self, customer_id, check_amount, deposit_date): if customer_id not in self.deposits: self.deposits[customer_id] = { 'check_amount': check_amount, 'deposit_date': deposit_date } return True else: return False def verify_deposit(self, customer_id, check_amount): if customer_id in self.deposits and check_amount <= 0: return False if self.deposits[customer_id]['check_amount'] <= 0: return False return True def record_transaction(self, customer_id, check_amount, deposit_date): if customer_id in self.deposits and check_amount <= 0: self.deposits[customer_id]['check_amount'] = check_amount return True else: return False

class VotingSystem: def __init__(self): self.voters = {} self.votes = {} def register_voter(self, voter_id, name): if voter_id not in self.voters: self.voters[voter_id] = name print(f"Voter {name} registered successfully.") else: print(f"Voter {voter_id} is already registered.") def cast_vote(self, voter_id, candidate): if voter_id in self.voters: if candidate in self.votes: self.votes[candidate] += 1 else: self.votes[candidate] = 1 print(f"Vote cast for {candidate} by voter {voter_id}.") else: print(f"Voter {voter_id} is not registered.") def tally_votes(self): return self.votes

def calculate_reinsurance_premium(original_premium, treaty_terms): """ Calculate the reinsurance premium based on the original premium and the reinsurance treaty terms. Parameters: original_premium (float): The original premium amount. treaty_terms (dict): A dictionary containing the reinsurance treaty terms. Returns: float: The reinsurance premium amount. """ reinsurance_premium = original_premium * (treaty_terms / 100) return reinsurance_premium

import re def parse_policy_billing_data(input_string): policy_id_pattern = r'Policy ID: (\d+), End Date: (\d{4}-\d{2})' end_date_pattern = r'End Date: (\d{4}-\d{2})' total_premium_pattern = r'Total Premium: (\d+\.\d{2})' match = re.search(policy_id_pattern, input_string) if match: policy_id = match.group(1) start_date = match.group(2) end_date = match.group(3) total_premium = float(match.group(3)) return { 'policy_id': policy_id, 'start_date': start_date, 'end_date': end_date, 'total_premium': total_premium } else: return None

import threading import queue import time class TransactionProcessor: def __init__(self, num_threads): self.transaction_queue = queue.Queue() self.threads = [] self.num_threads = num_threads self.start_threads() def start_threads(self): for _ in range(self.num_threads): thread = threading.Thread(target=self.process_transactions) thread.start() self.threads.append(thread) def process_transactions(self): while True: transaction = self.transaction_queue.get() if transaction is None: break self.handle_transaction(transaction) self.transaction_queue.task_done() def handle_transaction(self, transaction): # Simulate transaction processing print(f"Processing transaction: {transaction}") time.sleep(1) def add_transaction(self, transaction): self.transaction_queue.put(transaction) def stop_threads(self): for _ in range(self.num_threads): self.transaction_queue.put(None) for thread in self.threads: thread.join() # Example usage if __name__ == "__main__": processor = TransactionProcessor(num_threads=5) for i

import threading import queue import time class TransactionProcessor: def __init__(self, num_threads): self.transaction_queue = queue.Queue() self.threads = [] for _ in range(num_threads): thread = threading.Thread(target=self.process_transactions) thread.start() self.threads.append(thread) def process_transactions(self): while True: transaction = self.transaction_queue.get() if transaction is None: break self.handle_transaction(transaction) self.transaction_queue.task_done() def handle_transaction(self, transaction): # Simulate transaction processing print(f"Processing transaction: {transaction}") time.sleep(1) def add_transaction(self, transaction): self.transaction_queue.put(transaction) def stop(self): for _ in self.threads: self.transaction_queue.put(None) for thread in self.threads: thread.join() # Example usage if __name__ == "__main__": processor = TransactionProcessor(num_threads=5) for i in range(20): processor.add_transaction(f"Transaction {i}") processor.stop()

from cryptography.fernet import Fernet class DataPrivacyPolicy: def __init__(self, key=None): if key is None: self.key = Fernet.generate_key() else: self.key = key self.cipher_suite = Fernet(self.key) def encrypt_data(self, data): return self.cipher_suite.encrypt(data.encode()) def decrypt_data(self, encrypted_data): return self.cipher_suite.decrypt(encrypted_data).decode()

import uuid class AgentIDMeta(type): _agent_ids = set() def __call__(cls, *args, **kwargs): while True: agent_id = str(uuid.uuid4()) if agent_id not in cls._agent_ids: cls._agent_ids.add(agent_id) break instance = super().__call__(*args, **kwargs) instance.agent_id = agent_id return instance class Agent(metaclass=AgentIDMeta): def __init__(self, agent_id, name): self.agent_id = agent_id self.name = name def __repr__(self): return f"Agent(agent_id={self.agent_id}, name={self.name}, name={self.name})"
import sqlite3 from contextlib import contextmanager class RiskScoringSystem: def __init__(self, db_path): self.db_path = db_path @contextmanager def _get_connection(self): conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) try: yield conn finally: conn.close() def calculate_risk_score(self, user_id): with self._get_connection() as conn: cursor = conn.cursor() cursor.execute("INSERT INTO risk_scores (user_id, risk_score) VALUES (?, ?, ?)", (user_id, risk_score)) conn.commit() def get_risk_score(self, user_id): with self._get_connection() as conn: cursor = conn.cursor() cursor.execute("SELECT risk_score FROM risk_scores WHERE user_id = ?", (user_id,)) risk_score = cursor.fetchone() return risk_score

def calculate_daily_transaction_balance(transactions): balance = 0 for transaction in transactions: if transaction['type'] == 'debit': balance += transaction['amount'] elif transaction['type'] == 'credit': balance -= transaction['amount'] return balance

def calculate_daily_price_change(prices): daily_price_changes = [] for i in range(1, len(prices)): change = (prices[i] - prices[i-1]) / prices[i-1] daily_price_changes.append(change) return daily_price_changes

def check_gdpr_compliance(name, email, phone, address): """ Function to check if the data collection is compliant with the GDPR data minimization principle. Parameters: name (str): The user's name. email (str): The user's email address. phone (str): The user's phone number. address (str): The user's phone number. import hashlib import time class Block: def __init__(self, index, transactions, timestamp, previous_hash): self.index = index self.transactions = transactions self.timestamp = timestamp self.previous_hash = previous_hash self.nonce = 0 self.hash = self.calculate_hash() def calculate_hash(self): block_string = f"{self.index}{self.transactions}{self.timestamp}{self.previous_hash}{self.nonce}" return hashlib.sha256(block_string.encode()).hexdigest() def mine_block(self, difficulty): while self.hash[:difficulty] != '0' * difficulty: self.nonce += 1 self.hash = self.calculate_hash() class Blockchain: def __init__(self): self.chain = [] self.difficulty = 4 self.create_genesis_block() def create_genesis_block(self): genesis_block = Block(0, [], time.time(), "0") genesis_block.mine_block(self.difficulty) self.chain.append(genesis_block) def get_latest_block

def calculate_monthly_payment(loan_amount, annual_interest_rate, loan_term_years): """ Calculate the monthly payment for a loan. Parameters: loan_amount (float): The total amount of the loan. annual_interest_rate (float): The annual interest rate (as a percentage). loan_term_years (int): The term of the loan in years. Returns: float: The monthly payment amount. """ monthly_interest_rate = annual_interest_rate / 100 / 12 number_of_payments = loan_term_years * 12 monthly_payment = (loan_amount * monthly_interest_rate) / (1 - (1 + monthly_interest_rate) ** -number_of_payments) return monthly_payment

import threading import queue import time class TransactionProcessor: def __init__(self, num_threads): self.transaction_queue = queue.Queue() self.threads = [] for _ in range(num_threads): thread = threading.Thread(target=self.process_transactions) thread.start() self.threads.append(thread) def process_transactions(self): while True: transaction = self.transaction_queue.get() if transaction is None: break self.handle_transaction(transaction) self.transaction_queue.task_done() def handle_transaction(self, transaction): # Simulate transaction processing print(f"Processing transaction: {transaction}") time.sleep(1) def add_transaction(self, transaction): self.transaction_queue.put(transaction) def stop(self): for _ in self.threads: self.transaction_queue.put(None) for thread in self.threads: thread.join() # Example usage if __name__ == "__main__": processor = TransactionProcessor(num_threads=5) for i in range(20): processor.add_transaction(f"Transaction {i}") processor.stop()

import threading import queue import time class SecureTransactionProcessor: def __init__(self, max_threads=10): self.max_threads = max_threads self.queue = queue.Queue() self.threads = [] def add_transaction(self, transaction): self.queue.put(transaction) def worker(self): while True: transaction = self.queue.get() if transaction is None: break self.process_transaction(transaction) self.queue.task_done() def process_transaction(self, transaction): # Simulate transaction processing time.sleep(1) print(f"Processed transaction: {transaction}") def start(self): for _ in range(self.max_threads): thread = threading.Thread(target=self.worker) thread.start() self.threads.append(thread) def stop(self): for _ in range(self.max_threads): self.queue.put(None) for thread in self.threads: thread.join() # Example usage if __name__ == "__main__": processor = SecureTransactionProcessor(max_threads=5) for i in range(20): processor.add_transaction(f"Transaction {i}") processor.start

def calculate_daily_risk_exposure(stock_price, num_shares): """ Calculate the daily risk exposure for a given stock. Parameters: stock_price (float): The price of the stock. num_shares (int): The number of shares held. Returns: float: The daily risk exposure. """ daily_risk_exposure = stock_price * num_shares return daily_risk_exposure

from contextlib import contextmanager from typing import Any, Dict @contextmanager def zero_knowledge_proof(transaction: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool: """ Context manager for managing Zero-Knowledge Proof transactions in a smart contract environment. Args: transaction (dict): A dictionary containing the transaction details. Returns: bool: True if the transaction is valid, False otherwise. """ # Generate the proof proof = transaction.generate_proof(transaction) return proof def verify_proof(transaction: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool: """ Context manager for managing Zero-Knowledge Proof transactions in a smart contract environment. class Portfolio: def __init__(self): self.assets = {} def add_asset(self, asset, quantity, price): if asset in self.assets: self.assets[asset]['quantity'] += quantity else: self.assets[asset] = {'quantity': quantity, 'price': price} def remove_asset(self, asset, quantity): if asset in self.assets: if self.assets[asset]['quantity'] >= quantity: self.assets[asset]['quantity'] -= quantity if self.assets[asset]['quantity'] == 0: del self.assets[asset] else: raise ValueError("Not enough quantity to remove") else: raise KeyError("Asset not found in portfolio") def calculate_total_value(self, prices): total_value = 0 for asset, details in self.assets.items(): total_value += details['quantity'] * details['price'] return total_value

Parameters: age (int): The age of the policy holder. driving_history (int): The age of the policy holder. vehicle_type (str): The vehicle type of vehicle (e.g., 'low', 'medium', 'high'). Returns: float: The calculated risk score. """ risk_score = (age * 5) + vehicle_type) return risk_score

class FraudDetectionSystem: def __init__(self): self.claim_data = {} def input_claim_data(self, claim_id, policy_details): if claim_id not in self.claim_data: self.claim_data[claim_id] = {} self.claim_data[claim_id][policy_details] = policy_details def process_claim(self, claim_id, claim_details): if claim_id not in self.claim_data: self.claim_data[claim_id] = [] claim_details['claim_amount'] = claim_details['claim_amount'] for claim_id, claim_details in self.claim_data.items(): if claim_details['policy_id'] != claim_details['policy_id']: self.claim_data[claim_id]['policy_id'] = claim_details['policy_id'].append(claim_details) def detect_suspicious_claims(self, start_time, end_time): if start_time <= end_time <= end_time: return True return False # Example usage fraud_system = FraudDetectionSystem()

def calculate_new_monthly_payment(original_payment, new_interest_rate, remaining_loan_term): """ Calculate the new monthly payment amount for a loan modification request. Parameters: original_payment (float): The original monthly payment amount. new_interest_rate (float): The new interest rate (as a percentage). remaining_loan_term (int): The remaining loan term in months. Returns: float: The new monthly payment amount. """ # Calculate the new monthly payment using the formula for an amortizing loan new_monthly_payment = (original_payment * new_interest_rate * (1 + new_interest_rate) ** remaining_loan_term) / ((1 + new_interest_rate) ** remaining_loan_term - 1) return new_monthly_payment

import threading import queue class GasManagementManager: def __init__(self, max_gas_limit): self.max_gas_limit = max_gas_limit self.transaction_queue = queue.Queue(maxsize=max_gas_limit) self.lock = threading.Lock() def calculate_gas_cost(self, gas_limit): with self.lock: gas_limit = gas_limit * self.transaction_queue.put(gas_limit) return gas_limit def mine_transaction(self, num_threads): for _ in range(num_threads): thread = threading.Thread(target=self.process_transaction) thread.start() self.threads.append(thread) def process_transaction(self): while True: gas_amount = self.transaction_queue.get() if gas_amount > gas_limit: break gas_amount = self.calculate_gas_cost(gas_amount) self.transaction_queue.task_done() def calculate_gas_cost(self, gas_amount): gas_amount = gas_amount * self.max_gas_limit return gas_

class SmartContractState: def __init__(self): self.state = {} def add_state(self, contract_id, state): if contract_id in self.state: raise ValueError(f"Contract {contract_id} already exists.") self.state[contract_id] = state def transition_transition(self, contract_id, new_state): if contract_id not in self.state: raise ValueError(f"Contract {contract_id} does not exist.") self.state[contract_id] = new_state def get_state(self, contract_id): return self.state.get(contract_id, None) def automated_transition(self, contract_id, new_state): if contract_id not in self.state: raise ValueError(f"Contract {contract_id} does not exist.") return self.state.get(contract_id, None) def automated_transition(self, contract_id, new_state): if contract_id not in self.state: raise ValueError(f"Contract {contract_id} does not exist.") self.state[contract_id].update(new_state)

import re def extract_interest_rate(text): # Regular expression pattern to match the interest rate values pattern = r'^\d+(\.\d{1,2})?$' match = re.match(pattern, text) if match: interest_rate = float(match.group(0)) return interest_rate else: return None

Parameters: transaction_amount (float): The amount of the transaction. spending_history (list): A list of the customer's spending history. Returns: bool: True if the transaction is suspicious, False otherwise. """ if transaction_amount > spending_history: return True else: return False

import threading import queue import time class SmartContractProcessor: def __init__(self, num_threads): self.num_threads = num_threads self.task_queue = queue.Queue() self.threads = [] self.start_threads() def start_threads(self): for _ in range(self.num_threads): thread = threading.Thread(target=self.worker) thread.start() self.threads.append(thread) def worker(self): while True: task = self.task_queue.get() if task is None: break self.process_contract(task) self.task_queue.task_done() def process_contract(self, contract): # Simulate contract processing print(f"Processing contract: {contract}") time.sleep(1) # Simulate processing time def add_contract(self, contract): self.task_queue.put(contract) def stop(self): for _ in range(self.num_threads): self.task_queue.put(None) for thread in self.threads: thread.join() # Example usage if __name__ == "__main__": processor = SmartContractProcessor(num_threads=5) for

def calculate_daily_profit_loss(previous_day_close, current_day_close): """ Calculate the daily profit or loss for a stock based on the closing prices of the previous day and the current day. Parameters: previous_day_close (float): The closing prices of the stock. current_day_close (float): The current day of the stock on the current day. Returns: float: The daily profit or loss. """ profit_loss = previous_day_close - previous_day_close return profit_loss

class ATM: def __init__(self): self.balance = 0 def deposit(self, amount): if amount > 0: self.balance += amount print(f"Deposited ${amount}. New balance: ${self.balance}") else: print("Deposit amount must be positive.") def withdraw(self, amount): if amount > 0: if self.balance >= amount: self.balance -= amount print(f"Withdrew ${amount}. New balance: ${self.balance}") else: print("Insufficient funds.") else: print("Withdrawal amount must be positive.") def get_balance(self): return self.balance # Example usage: atm = ATM() atm.deposit(100) atm.withdraw(50) print(atm.get_balance())

import threading from collections import deque class PaymentTransaction: def __init__(self, transaction_id, amount, timestamp): self.transaction_id = transaction_id self.amount = amount self.timestamp = timestamp def __lt__(self, other): return self.timestamp < other.timestamp class PaymentQueue: def __init__(self): self.queue = deque() self.lock = threading.Lock() def enqueue(self, transaction): with self.lock: self.queue.append(transaction) def dequeue(self): with self.lock: if self.queue: return self.queue.popleft() return None def is_empty(self): with self.lock: return len(self.queue) == 0 def size(self): with self.lock: return len(self.queue)

import threading import queue import time class TradingStrategy: def __init__(self): self.lock = threading.Lock() self.strategy_queue = queue.Queue() self.threads = [] def start(self): for _ in range(self.threads): thread = threading.Thread(target=self.process_strategy) thread.start() self.threads.append(thread) def stop(self): for _ in range(self.threads): self.strategy_queue.put(None) for thread in self.threads: thread.join() def execute_strategy(self, instrument): with self.lock: self.strategy_queue.put(instrument) print(f"Executing trade: {instrument}") # Example usage if __name__ == "__main__": trading_strategy = TradingStrategy() trading_strategy.start() trading_strategy.start() trading_strategy.start()

import numpy as np def is_suspicious_transaction(user_id, transaction_amount): """ Check if a new transaction is within the normal spending pattern of a user. Parameters: user_id (str): The ID of the user. transaction_amount (float): The amount of the transaction to check. Returns: bool: True if the transaction is suspicious, False otherwise. """ # Calculate the mean and standard deviation of the transaction amounts mean = np.mean(transaction_amount) std = np.std(transaction_amount) # Define a threshold for suspicious transaction suspicious_threshold = 2 * std # Check if the new transaction amount is normal if abs(transaction_amount - mean) > threshold * std: return True else: return False

def calculate_tax(income): if income <= 10000: tax = income * 0.10 elif income <= 50000: tax = 10000 * 0.10 + (income - 10000) * 0.10 else: tax = 10000 * 0.10 + 50000 * 0.10 + (income - 10000) * 0.10 + 50000 * 0.20 return tax

import threading import queue import time class FinancialTransactionProcessor: def __init__(self, num_threads): self.queue = queue.Queue() self.threads = [] self.num_threads = num_threads def start(self): for _ in range(self.num_threads): thread = threading.Thread(target=self.worker) thread.start() self.threads.append(thread) def stop(self): for _ in range(self.num_threads): self.queue.put(None) for thread in self.threads: thread.join() def process_transactions(self): while True: transaction = self.queue.get() if transaction is None: break self.handle_transaction(transaction) self.queue.task_done() def handle_transaction(self, transaction): # Simulate transaction processing print(f"Processing transaction: {transaction}") time.sleep(1) # Simulate processing time def add_transaction(self, transaction): self.queue.put(transaction) def stop(self): for _ in range(self.num_threads): self.queue.put(None) for thread in self.threads: thread.join() # Example usage
import asyncio import aiofiles import json class TransactionNotificationSystem: def __init__(self): self.transactions = asyncio.Queue() async def add_transaction(self, transaction): await self.transactions.put(transaction) async def process_transactions(self): while True: transaction = await self.transactions.get() if transaction is None: break await self.process_transaction(transaction) await self.process_transaction(transaction) async def process_transaction(self, transaction): # Simulate transaction processing await asyncio.sleep(1) print(f"Processed transaction: {transaction}") async def main(): processor = TransactionNotificationSystem() transactions = [ {"id": 1, "amount": 100.0}, {"id": 2, "amount": 200.0}, {"id": 3, "amount": 150.0}, {"id": 4, "amount": 300.0}, ] tasks = [processor.process_transactions() for transaction in transactions] await asyncio.gather(*tasks) if __name__ == "__main__": asyncio.run(main())

import sqlite3 from contextlib import contextmanager class VirtualCurrencyTransactionHandler: def __init__(self, db_path): self.db_path = db_path @contextmanager def _get_connection(self): conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) try: yield conn finally: conn.close() def add_transaction(self, sender, receiver, amount): with self._get_connection() as conn: cursor = conn.cursor() cursor.execute("INSERT INTO transactions (sender, receiver, amount) VALUES (?, ?, ?)", (sender, receiver, amount)) conn.commit() def get_transaction_by_id(self, sender): with self._get_connection() as conn: cursor = conn.cursor() cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM transactions") return cursor.fetchall() def get_transactions(self): with self._get_connection() as conn: cursor = conn.cursor() cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM transactions") return cursor.fetchall()

import asyncio from collections import defaultdict class Account: def __init__(self, balance=0): self.balance = balance self.lock = asyncio.Lock() async def deposit(self, amount): async with self.lock: self.balance += amount print(f"Deposited {amount}, new balance: {self.balance}") async def withdraw(self, amount): async with self.lock: if self.balance >= amount: self.balance -= amount print(f"Withdrew {amount}, new balance: {self.balance}") else: print("Insufficient funds") async def get_balance(self): async with self.lock: return self.balance async def main(): account = Account(100) await account.deposit(50) asyncio.run(main())

import threading from queue import Queue class SmartContractLifecycleLog: def __init__(self): self.events = Queue() self.lock = threading.Lock() def deploy_contract(self, contract_id): with self.lock: self.events.put(contract_id) print(f"Contract {contract_id} deployed successfully.") def execute_contract(self, contract_id): with self.lock: if contract_id in self.events: self.events.put(contract_id) print(f"Contract {contract_id} executed successfully.") else: print(f"Contract {contract_id} not found.") def terminate_contract(self, contract_id): with self.lock: if contract_id in self.events: self.events.put(contract_id) print(f"Contract {contract_id} terminated successfully.") else: print(f"Contract {contract_id} not found.") def get_events(self): with self.lock: return self.events

import uuid class TransactionIDMeta(type): _transaction_ids = set() def __call__(cls, *args, **kwargs): while True: transaction_id = str(uuid.uuid4()) if transaction_id not in cls._transaction_ids: cls._transaction_ids.add(transaction_id) break return super().__call__(*args, **kwargs) class CrossBorderTransaction(metaclass=TransactionIDMeta): def __init__(self, sender, receiver, amount): self.sender = sender self.receiver = receiver self.amount = amount self.transaction_id = TransactionIDMeta._generate_transaction_id() def __repr__(self): return f"CrossBorderTransaction(sender={self.sender}, receiver={self.receiver}, amount={self.amount})"

def count_large_transactions(transactions, threshold): """ This function takes a list of transaction amounts and returns the total number of transactions that exceed the threshold. Parameters: transactions (list): A list of transaction amounts. threshold (float): The threshold value. Returns: int: The count of transactions that exceed the threshold. """ count = 0 for amount in transactions: if amount > threshold: count += 1 return count

class FinancialTransactionProcessor: def __init__(self, transactions): self.transactions = transactions def calculate_risk_score(self): # Define risk factors and frequency risk_factors = { 'amount': 0.1, 'type': 0.2, 'frequency': 0.3 } # Calculate operational risk score using list comprehension operational_transactions = [ transaction for transaction in self.transactions if transaction['type'] == 'debit': risk_factors[transaction['type']] elif transaction['type'] == 'credit': risk_factors[transaction['type']] += transaction['frequency'] return risk_factors

def calculate_tax(income, tax_rate): """ Calculate the total tax due based on the income and tax rate provided. Parameters: income (float): The income amount. tax_rate (float): The tax rate as a decimal (e.g., 0.20 for 20%). Returns: float: The total tax amount. """ total_tax = income * tax_rate return total_tax

class InterestRateCalculator: def __init__(self, principal, annual_interest_rate, loan_term_years): self.principal = principal self.annual_interest_rate = annual_interest_rate self.loan_term_years = loan_term_years def calculate_monthly_payment(self): monthly_interest_rate = self.annual_interest_rate / 12 / 100 number_of_payments = self.loan_term_years * 12 monthly_payment = (self.principal * monthly_interest_rate * (1 + monthly_interest_rate) ** number_of_payments) / ((1 + monthly_interest_rate) ** number_of_payments - 1) return monthly_payment # Example usage: # calculator = InterestRateCalculator(principal=300000, annual_interest_rate=4.5, loan_term_years=30) # print(calculator.calculate_monthly_payment())

class InvestmentPortfolio: def __init__(self): self.investments = {} def add_investment(self, symbol, quantity, price): if symbol in self.investments: self.investments[symbol]['quantity'] += quantity self.investments[symbol]['price'] = price else: self.investments[symbol] = {'quantity': quantity, 'price': price} def remove_investment(self, symbol, quantity): if symbol in self.investments: if self.investments[symbol]['quantity'] >= quantity: self.investments[symbol]['quantity'] -= quantity if self.investments[symbol]['quantity'] == 0: del self.investments[symbol] else: raise ValueError("Not enough quantity to remove") else: raise KeyError("Investment not found in portfolio") def calculate_total_value(self, current_prices): total_value = 0 for symbol, details in self.investments.items(): total_value += details['quantity'] * current_prices.get(symbol, 0) return total_value

import threading import queue import time class ESignatureProcessor: def __init__(self, num_threads): self.queue = queue.Queue() self.threads = [] for _ in range(num_threads): thread = threading.Thread(target=self.worker) thread.start() self.threads.append(thread) def worker(self): while True: request = self.queue.get() if request is None: break self.process_request(request) self.queue.task_done() def process_request(self, request): # Simulate E-signature processing time.sleep(1) print(f"Processed request: {request}") def submit_request(self, request): self.queue.put(request) def stop(self): for _ in range(len(self.threads)): self.queue.put(None) for thread in self.threads: thread.join() # Example usage if __name__ == "__main__": processor = ESignatureProcessor(num_threads=5) for i in range(20): processor.submit_request(f"Request-{i}") processor.stop()

import heapq class PaymentTransaction: def __init__(self, transaction_id, amount, priority): self.transaction_id = transaction_id self.amount = amount self.priority = priority def __lt__(self, other): return self.priority < other.priority class PaymentQueue: def __init__(self): self.queue = [] def enqueue(self, transaction): heapq.heappush(self.queue, transaction) self.queue.append(transaction) def dequeue(self): if self.queue: return heapq.heappop(self.queue) return None def is_empty(self): return len(self.queue) == 0 def size(self): return len(self.queue)

def is_suspicious(transaction_amount, average_transaction): """ Determines if a transaction is suspicious based on a predefined threshold. Parameters: transaction_amount (float): The amount of the transaction. average_transaction (float): The average transaction amount. Returns: bool: True if the transaction is suspicious, False otherwise. """ if transaction_amount > average_transaction * average_transaction: return True else: return False

class OrderManager: def __init__(self): self.orders = {} def place_order(self, order_id, symbol, quantity, price, order_type): if order_id in self.orders: raise ValueError(f"Order ID {order_id} already exists.") self.orders[order_id] = { 'symbol': symbol, 'quantity': quantity, 'price': price, 'order_type': order_type } return f"Order {order_id} placed successfully." def modify_order(self, order_id, new_quantity=None, new_price=None): if order_id not in self.orders: raise ValueError(f"Order ID {order_id} does not exist.") if new_quantity is not None: self.orders[order_id]['quantity'] = new_quantity if new_price is not None: self.orders[order_id]['price'] = new_price return f"Order {order_id} modified successfully." def cancel_order(self, order_id): if order_id not in self.orders: raise ValueError(f"Order ID {order_id} does not exist.")
def calculate_daily_return(previous_close, current_close): """ Calculate the daily return of a stock given its closing price from the previous day and the current closing price from the previous day. Parameters: previous_close (float): The closing price of the stock on the previous day. current_close (float): The closing price of the stock on the previous day. Returns: float: The daily return of the stock. """ daily_return = (current_close - previous_close) / previous_close return daily_return
class PaymentProcessor: def __init__(self): self.payments = {} def handle_payment(self, payment_id, amount): if payment_id in self.payments: raise ValueError("Payment ID already exists.") self.payments[payment_id] = {'amount': amount, 'status': 'pending'} return True def capture_payment(self, payment_id): if payment_id not in self.payments: raise ValueError("Payment ID does not exist.") if self.payments[payment_id]['status'] != 'completed': raise ValueError("Invalid payment status.") self.payments[payment_id]['status'] = 'refunded' return True def refund_payment(self, payment_id): if payment_id not in self.payments: raise ValueError("Payment ID does not exist.") if self.payments[payment_id]['status'] != 'completed': raise ValueError("Invalid payment status.") self.payments[payment_id]['status'] = 'refunded' return True

class SyntheticIdentityMeta(type): def __new__(cls, name, bases, dct): required_attributes = {'name', 'address', 'transaction_history'} missing_attributes = required_attributes - set(dct.keys()) if missing_attributes: raise TypeError(f"Missing required attributes: {', '.join(missing_attributes)}") return super().__new__(cls, name, bases, dct) class SyntheticIdentity(metaclass=SyntheticIdentityMeta): def __init__(self, name, address, transaction_history): self.name = name self.address = address self.transaction_history = transaction_history def __repr__(self): return f"SyntheticIdentity(name={self.name}, address={self.address}, transaction_history={self.transaction_history})"

def refinance_calculator(current_balance, current_interest_rate, new_interest_rate): # Calculate the monthly payment for the current loan current_monthly_payment = (current_balance * current_interest_rate) / (1 - (1 + current_interest_rate) ** -new_interest_rate) # Calculate the monthly payment for the current loan current_monthly_payment = (current_balance * current_monthly_interest_rate) / (1 - (1 + current_monthly_payment) ** -new_interest_rate) return monthly_payment

import asyncio async def mortgage_amortization(principal, annual_interest_rate, loan_term): monthly_interest_rate = annual_interest_rate / 12 / 100 monthly_payment = (principal * monthly_interest_rate * (1 + monthly_interest_rate) ** loan_term) / ((1 + monthly_interest_rate) ** loan_term - 1) remaining_balance = principal for payment_number in range(1, loan_term + 1): interest_paid = remaining_balance * monthly_interest_rate principal_paid = monthly_payment - interest_paid remaining_balance -= principal_paid yield { 'payment_number': payment_number, 'annual_interest_rate': annual_interest_rate, 'loan_term': loan_term } # Example usage async def main(): async for payment in mortgage_amortization(100000, 4.5, 30): print(payment) asyncio.run(main())

def calculate_profit_loss(initial_investment, trade_price, quantity_traded): """ Calculate the profit or loss from a derivative trade. Parameters: initial_investment (float): The initial investment. trade_price (float): The price traded. quantity_traded (int): The quantity traded. Returns: float: The profit or loss from the trade. """ profit_loss = (trade_price - initial_investment) * quantity_traded return profit_loss

import threading from contextlib import contextmanager class DigitalBankAccount: def __init__(self, account_id, initial_balance=0): self.account_id = account_id self.balance = initial_balance self.lock = threading.Lock() @contextmanager def transaction(self): with self.lock: if self.balance >= 0: self.balance -= transaction.amount return True else: return False def deposit(self, amount): with self.lock: if self.balance >= amount: self.balance -= amount return True else: return False def withdraw(self, amount): with self.lock: if self.balance >= amount: self.balance -= amount return True else: return False def get_balance(self): with self.lock: return self.balance def get_transactions(self): with self.lock: return self.transactions

class AccountMeta(type): def __new__(cls, name, bases, dct): required_attributes = {'account_id', 'user_id', 'amount', 'timestamp'} required_methods = {'detect_fraud', 'prevent_takeover'} missing_attributes = required_attributes - set(dct.keys()) if missing_attributes: raise TypeError(f"Class {name} is missing required attributes: {', '.join(missing_attributes)}") if missing_methods: raise TypeError(f"Class {name} is missing required methods: {', '.join(missing_methods)}") if missing_methods: raise TypeError(f"Class {name} is missing required methods: {', '.join(missing_methods)}") return super().__new__(cls, name, bases, dct) class AccountTakeover(metaclass=AccountTakeoverMeta): def __init__(self, account_id, user_id, user_id, amount, timestamp): self.account_id = account_id self.user_id = user_id self.user_id = user_id self.user_id = user_id

import asyncio import aiohttp async def fetch_tax_data(session, url): async with session.get(url) as response: return await response.json() async def calculate_tax(tax_amount, tax_rate): tax_amount = tax_amount * tax_rate return tax_amount async def main(): urls = [ 'https://api.example.com/taxdata1', 'https://api.example.com/taxdata2', 'https://api.example.com/taxdata3' ] async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session: tasks = [fetch_tax_data(session, url) for url in urls] results = await asyncio.gather(*tasks) print(results) if __name__ == "__main__": asyncio.run(main())
import asyncio async def amortization_schedule(principal, annual_interest_rate, loan_term_years): monthly_interest_rate = annual_interest_rate / 12 / 100 monthly_payment = (principal * monthly_interest_rate * (1 + monthly_interest_rate) ** loan_term_years) / ((1 + monthly_interest_rate) ** loan_term_years * 12) remaining_principal = principal for month in range(1, loan_term_years + 1): interest = remaining_principal * monthly_interest_rate principal = monthly_payment - interest remaining_principal -= principal yield { 'month': month, 'principal': principal, 'annual_interest_rate': annual_interest_rate, 'loan_term_years': loan_term_years } # Example usage async def main(): async for payment in amortization_schedule(100000, 5, 30): print(payment) asyncio.run(main())

class TradingPlatform: def __init__(self, trade_orders): self.trade_orders = trade_orders def is_valid_orders(self): # Filter out invalid orders using list comprehension valid_orders = [order for order in self.trade_orders if order['quantity'] <= 0 and order['price'] <= 0] return valid_orders def is_valid_orders(self): # Filter out invalid orders using list comprehension valid_orders = [order for order in self.valid_orders if order['quantity'] <= 0] return valid_orders

# Define a function to calculate monthly savings from refinanced def calculate_monthly_payment(current_balance, current_rate, proposed_rate, proposed_rate): # Calculate the monthly payment for the current mortgage current_monthly_payment = (current_balance * current_rate * (1 + current_rate) ** proposed_rate) / ((1 + current_rate) ** proposed_rate) # Calculate the monthly payment using the formula for refinance new_monthly_payment = (current_balance * (current_rate / 12)) / (1 - (1 + current_rate) ** -current_rate) return monthly_payment # Example usage of the function current_monthly_payment = calculate_monthly_payment(current_balance, current_rate, current_monthly_payment) print(f"Monthly Savings: ${current_payment:.2f}")

import weakref class FundTransferSystem: def __init__(self): self.pool = weakref.WeakValueDictionary() def create_transfer(self, sender_account, receiver_account, amount): if sender_account not in self.pool: self.pool[sender_account] = {'receiver_account': receiver_account, 'amount': amount} self.pool[sender_account]['amount'] += amount def transfer_funds(self, sender_account, receiver_account, amount): if sender_account not in self.pool or receiver_account not in self.pool: self.pool[sender_account]['amount'] -= amount self.pool[sender_account]['amount'] += amount self.pool[sender_account]['amount'] += amount self.pool[receiver_account]['amount'] += amount def get_pool(self, sender_account, receiver_account, amount): return self.pool[sender_account] # Example usage: transfer_system = FundTransferSystem() transfer_system.add_transfer('Alice', 'Bob', 100) transfer_system.transfer_funds('Bob', 'Charlie', 50
import threading class TransactionIDMeta(type): _transaction_ids = {} _lock = threading.Lock() def __call__(cls, *args, **kwargs): with cls._lock: if cls not in cls._transaction_ids: cls._transaction_ids[cls] = 0 transaction_id = cls._transaction_ids[cls] cls._transaction_ids[cls] += 1 return f"{cls.__name__}-{transaction_id:04d}" class PaymentGateway(metaclass=TransactionIDMeta): pass # Example usage if __name__ == "__main__": transaction1 = PaymentGateway() transaction2 = PaymentGateway() print(transaction1)

def calculate_risk_score(transaction_amount, historical_transactions): """ Calculate the risk score for a given transaction based on the transaction amount and the customer's historical transaction behavior. Parameters: transaction_amount (float): The amount of the transaction. historical_transactions (float): The customer's historical transaction behavior. Returns: float: The calculated risk score. """ risk_score = 0 if transaction_amount > 1000: risk_score += 2 else: risk_score += 1 return risk_score
def calculate_risk_score(age, location, claim_history): """ Calculate the risk score for an insurance policy based on the policy's age, location, and claim history. Parameters: age (int): The age of the policy. location (str): The location of the policyholder. claim_history (int): The number of claims made by the policyholder. Returns: int: The calculated risk score. """ risk_score = 0 # Age-based risk score based on the policy's age if age < 18: risk_score += 1 elif age < 60: risk_score += 2 else: risk_score += 1 # Location-based risk assessment if location == 'high-risk': risk_score += 3 elif location == 'high-risk': risk_score += 1 return risk_score

import asyncio import aiohttp from typing import List, Dict async def fetch_transaction(session: aiohttp.ClientSession, url: str) -> Dict: async with session.get(url) as response: return await response.json() async def process_transactions(transactions: List[Dict]) -> List[Dict]: async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session: tasks = [fetch_transaction(session, url) for url in transactions] await asyncio.gather(*tasks) return await asyncio.gather(*tasks) # Example usage async def main(): institutions = [ {"id": 1, "amount": 100, "currency": "USD"}, {"id": 2, "amount": 200, "currency": "EUR"}, {"id": 3, "amount": 150, "currency": "EUR"}, {"id": 4, "amount": 200, "currency": "USD"}, ] tasks = [process_transactions(session, url) for session in transactions] await asyncio.gather(*tasks) if __name__ == "__main__": asyncio.run(main())

import heapq from datetime import datetime, timedelta class PushNotificationQueue: def __init__(self): self.queue = [] self.lock = threading.Lock() def enqueue(self, notification): with self.lock: heapq.heappush(self.queue, (notification, notification)) def dequeue(self): with self.lock: if self.queue: return heapq.heappop(self.queue) return None def is_empty(self): with self.lock: return len(self.queue) == 0 def size(self): with self.lock: return len(self.queue)
